Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the 3d space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 547 and the convergence rate is O(594/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_547(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 547. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^594(S, F) is trivial. QED.
